KOR_saudi_volunteer_setup_effect = {
	# 처음 시작할 때 이거 넣어야 함
	KOR = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_saudi_volunteer_dynamic } }
	set_variable = { KOR_saudi_deployed_troops = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_saudi_casualties = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_saudi_dissatisfaction = 0.2 }
	set_variable = { KOR_saudi_dissatisfaction_USA = 0.8 }
	KOR_saudi_volunteer_modifier_effect = yes
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_owned_by = SAU
				is_owned_by = ROC
				is_owned_by = ARR
				is_owned_by = SAR
				is_owned_by = JAH
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_saudi_debuff_states_dynamic }
	}
}
KOR_saudi_volunteer_modifier_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_saudi_volunteer_stability_factor = KOR.KOR_saudi_dissatisfaction }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_saudi_volunteer_stability_factor = -1 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_saudi_volunteer_stability_factor = 0.2 }
	set_variable = { KOR_saudi_volunteer_war_support_factor = KOR.KOR_saudi_dissatisfaction_USA }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_saudi_volunteer_war_support_factor = -1 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_saudi_volunteer_war_support_factor = 0.2 }
}

	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_saudi_dissatisfaction_change_tooltip

}

KOR_coronavirus_setup_effect = {
	KOR = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_coronavirus_dynamic }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_dynamic }
	}
	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var = 4 }
	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var = 5 }
	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee = 0.03 }
	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_fatality = 0.05 }
	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_deaths_number = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_timeout_days = 50 }
	KOR_coronavirus_modifier_effect = yes
}

KOR_coronavirus_modifier_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var }
	divide_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var = 100 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var = 0.01 }

	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_stability_factor = KOR.KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_stability_factor = -1 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_stability_factor = 0.003 }

	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_political_power_gain = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_political_power_gain = -1 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_political_power_gain = KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var }

	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_stability_factor = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_stability_factor = -0.4 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_stability_factor = KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var }

	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_expense_growth_factor = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_expense_growth_factor = 0.3 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_expense_growth_factor = KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var }

	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_society_development_monthl = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_society_development_monthl = -0.075 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_society_development_monthl = KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var }

	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_industrial_capacity_factory = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_industrial_capacity_factory = -0.4 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_industrial_capacity_factory = KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var }

	set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_income_growth_factor = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_income_growth_factor = -0.4 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_income_growth_factor = KOR_coronavirus_modifier_var }
}

KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee = KOR_coronavirus_infectee_temp }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_coronavirus_infectee min = 0 max = 5000 }
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_tooltip
}

KOR_coronavirus_infection_var_change_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var = KOR_coronavirus_infection_temp }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var min = 0 max = 100 }
	KOR_coronavirus_modifier_effect = yes
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_coronavirus_infection_var_change_tooltip
	if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched
				check_variable = { KOR.KOR_coronavirus_infection_var = 0 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { country_event = korea.4 }
	}
}

KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_var_change_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_var = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_temp }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_var min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_coronavirus_fatality min = 0 max = 1 }
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_var_change_tooltip
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_var = 100 } }
		hidden_effect = { country_event = korea.3 }
	}
}

KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var_change_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var = KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var_temp }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var min = 0 max = 100 }
	KOR_coronavirus_modifier_effect = yes
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var_change_tooltip
}

KOR_coronavirus_infectee_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var = 0 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee_temp = 5 }
		KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var > 0 }
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var < 26 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee_temp = 10 }
		KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var > 25 }
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var < 51 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee_temp = 50 }
		KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var > 51 }
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var < 76 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee_temp = 150 }
		KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_var > 76 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee_temp = 300 }
		KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_effect = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched }
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infectee_temp = -50 }
		KOR_coronavirus_infectee_change_effect = yes
	}
}

KOR_decrease_oil_shock_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_oil_shock4
		}
		remove_ideas = KOR_oil_shock4
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_oil_shock3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = KOR_oil_shock3
			add_idea = KOR_oil_shock4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_oil_shock2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = KOR_oil_shock2
			add_idea = KOR_oil_shock3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_oil_shock1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = KOR_oil_shock1
			add_idea = KOR_oil_shock2
		}
	}
}

KOR_president_election_setup_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_yoon_support = 0.5 }
	set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_lee_support = 0.5 }
}

KOR_president_election_calculate_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { president_faction_target = 1 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_yoon_support_temp = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_lee_support = KOR_president_election_yoon_support_temp }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { president_faction_target = 2 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_lee_support_temp = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_yoon_support = KOR_president_election_lee_support_temp }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { president_election_total_popularity = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_president_election_yoon_support min = 0 max = 1 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { total_popularity = KOR_president_election_yoon_support }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_president_election_lee_support min = 0 max = 1 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { total_popularity = KOR_president_election_lee_support }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { president_election_total_popularity > 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { scale_factor_percent = 100 }
		divide_temp_variable = { scale_factor_percent = president_election_total_popularity }

		multiply_variable = { KOR_president_election_yoon_support = scale_factor_percent }
		divide_variable = { KOR_president_election_yoon_support = 100 }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { president_election_total_popularity = KOR_president_election_yoon_support }
		set_variable = { KOR_president_election_lee_support = president_election_total_popularity }

		set_temp_variable = { final_check_sum = KOR_president_election_yoon_support }

		add_to_temp_variable = { final_check_sum = KOR_president_election_yoon_support }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { final_check_sum < 100 } }
			set_temp_variable = { remainder = 100 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { remainder = final_check_sum }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_yoon_support = remainder }
		}
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_yoon_support_temp = -1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_lee_support_temp = -1 }
}

KOR_president_election_yoon_support_change_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { president_faction_target = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_yoon_support = KOR_president_election_yoon_support_temp }
	KOR_president_election_calculate_effect = yes
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_president_election_yoon_support_change_tooltip
}

KOR_president_election_lee_support_change_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { president_faction_target = 2 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_lee_support = KOR_president_election_lee_support_temp }
	KOR_president_election_calculate_effect = yes
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_president_election_lee_support_change_tooltip
}

KOR_president_election_2027_setup_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = 0.5 }
	set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support = 0.5 }
	KOR_president_election_2027_inlay_effect = yes
}

KOR_president_election_2027_inlay_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_left_var = KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_left_var = 100 }
	round_variable = KOR_president_election_2027_left_var
	set_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_right_var = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_right_var = 100 }
	round_variable = KOR_president_election_2027_right_var
}

KOR_president_election_2027_calculate_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { president_faction_target = 1 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_temp = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_temp }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { president_faction_target = 2 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support_temp = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support_temp }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { president_election_total_popularity = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support min = 0 max = 1 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { total_popularity = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support min = 0 max = 1 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { total_popularity = KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { president_election_total_popularity > 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { scale_factor_percent = 100 }
		divide_temp_variable = { scale_factor_percent = president_election_total_popularity }

		multiply_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = scale_factor_percent }
		divide_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = 100 }

		subtract_from_temp_variable = { president_election_total_popularity = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support }
		set_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support = president_election_total_popularity }

		set_temp_variable = { final_check_sum = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support }

		add_to_temp_variable = { final_check_sum = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { final_check_sum < 100 } }
			set_temp_variable = { remainder = 100 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { remainder = final_check_sum }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = remainder }
		}
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_temp = -1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support_temp = -1 }
}

KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_change_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { president_faction_target = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_temp }
	KOR_president_election_2027_calculate_effect = yes
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_change_tooltip
	KOR_president_election_2027_inlay_effect = yes
}

add_temp_unit_leader_role = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_unit_leader = no }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				skill = 1
				traits = { just_visiting }
				visible = { always = no }
			}
			set_character_flag = temp_unit_leader
		}
	}
}

remove_temp_unit_leader_role = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = temp_unit_leader }
			remove_unit_leader_role = yes
			clr_character_flag = temp_unit_leader
		}
	}
}

KOR_aging_population_good_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_2
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_3
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_4
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_5
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_4
		}
		country_event = korea.1565
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_5
		}
		add_political_power = 150
		add_stability = 0.1
	}
}

KOR_aging_population_bad_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea
		}
		add_political_power = -150
		add_stability = -0.1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_2
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_3
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_5
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			add_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_4
			remove_idea = KOR_aging_population_idea_5
		}
	}
}
KOR_aging_population_check_up_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_korea_no_sex_var = KOR_korea_no_sex_var_temp }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_korea_no_sex_var min = 0 max = 50 }
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_korea_no_sex_effect_change_tooltip
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { KOR_korea_no_sex_var = 10 }
				check_variable = { KOR_korea_no_sex_var = 20 }
				check_variable = { KOR_korea_no_sex_var = 30 }
				check_variable = { KOR_korea_no_sex_var = 40 }
				check_variable = { KOR_korea_no_sex_var = 50 }
			}
		}
		KOR_aging_population_good_effect = yes
	}
}

	}

			remove_idea = KOR_china_sanctions_idea1

	else_if = {

		}

			remove_idea = KOR_china_sanctions_idea
		}

		add_industrial_development = yes
	}

KOR_add_to_pdto_effect = {
	set_country_flag = pdto_member
	if = {
		limit = {
			JAP = {
				has_completed_focus = JAP_reinforce_pdto
				NOT = {
					has_completed_focus = JAP_continue_military_integration
				}
			}
		}
		add_ideas = JAP_PDTO_member_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			JAP = {
				has_completed_focus = JAP_reinforce_pdto
				has_completed_focus = JAP_continue_military_integration
			}
		}
		add_ideas = JAP_PDTO_member_2
	}
}
KOR_LJS_clamp_approval_rating_to_cap_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_cap_var
			value = party_popularity@social_liberal
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_cap_var
			value = -50
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_cap_var
			value = 90
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
			min = 0
			max = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_cap_var
		}
	}
}

KOR_LJS_approval_rating_much = {  ##이거 작동하면 내가 평소에 덕을 많이 쌓은 것이 분명함
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi
			value = 90
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi_minju
			value = party_popularity@social_liberal
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi_minju
			value = 50
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi_min
			value = party_popularity@market_liberal
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi_min
			value = 100
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi
			value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi_minju
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi_min min = 0 max = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi }
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi1
			value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi1
			value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi1
			value = 0.10368
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
			value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi1
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var min = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi_min max = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_imsi }
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_much_tooltip
}

KOR_LJS_liberal_down_auto = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			value = 1
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			value = party_popularity@market_liberal
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_2
			value = party_popularity@conservative
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			value = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_2
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_party_support_floor_temp = 0.3 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				KOR_LJS_jeong_cheong_rae_ending_locked = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_LJS_party_support_floor_temp = 0.15 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1 > KOR_LJS_party_support_floor_temp
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
				value = KOR_LJS_party_support_floor_temp
		    }
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
				value = 0.02
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
				value = -1
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = market_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_3
				value = 0.5
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_3
				value = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_3
				value = 0.01
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = market_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_3
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_3
			}
		}
	}

			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
		}
		else = {
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
				value = 0.028
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = market_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
				value = -0.7
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
		}

KOR_LJS_ruling_up_1 = { #그냥 여당 지지율 높이기(조금)
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			value = 0.7
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			value = party_popularity@market_liberal
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_2
			value = party_popularity@conservative
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
			value = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_2
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			value = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1 < 0.4
				}
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
				value = 0.012
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = market_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi_1 < 0.5
				}
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
				value = 0.02
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = market_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
		}
		else = {
			multiply_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
				value = 0.024
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = market_liberal
				popularity = KOR_LJS_popularity_imsi
			}
		}
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = LJS_popularity_ppp_up_tt_1
}

KOR_documents_add_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = Documents_button_enable }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_documents_enable_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		set_country_flag = Documents_button_enable
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { KOR_top_secret_documents = documents_id } }
		}
		add_to_array = { KOR_top_secret_documents = documents_id }
	}
	meta_effect = {
		text = { custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_Documents_[ID]_tt }
		ID = "[?documents_id]"
	}
}

KOR_documents_remove_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KOR_top_secret_documents = documents_id } }
		remove_from_array = { array = KOR_top_secret_documents value = documents_id }
	}
	meta_effect = {
		text = { custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_Documents_[ID]_remove_tt }
		ID = "[?documents_id]"
	}
}

KOR_army_nuclear_program_init = {
	set_temp_variable = { documents_id = 2 }
	KOR_documents_add_effect = yes
	set_variable = { KOR_set_nuclear_budget = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR.army_nuclear_progress = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_army_nuclear_cost = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_army_nuclear_factory_cost = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_nuclear_monthly_speed = 0 }
	hidden_effect = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_army_nuclear_cost_dynamic }
	}
}

KOR_nuclear_program_change_progress = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR.army_nuclear_progress = army_nuclear_progress_temp } # use this in effects
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { army_nuclear_progress_temp < 0 }
		}
		multiply_variable = { army_nuclear_progress_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_army_nuclear_program_decrease_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_army_nuclear_program_increase_tt
	}
	add_to_variable = { TFR_KOR_Army_Nuclear_Program_dirty = 1 }
}

KOR_army_nuclear_development_increase = {
	add_to_variable = {
		KOR.army_nuclear_progress = {
			value = KOR_nuclear_monthly_speed
			clamp = { min = 0 max = 10 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = KOR.army_nuclear_progress
				value = 10
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_ready_to_launch_nuclear
		country_event = korea.1135
	}
	add_to_variable = { TFR_KOR_Army_Nuclear_Program_dirty = 1 }
}

KOR_army_change_nuclear_budget = {
	# set_variable = { KOR_set_nuclear_budget = amount?KOR_set_nuclear_budget }
	set_variable = {
		KOR_nuclear_monthly_speed = {
			value = KOR_set_nuclear_budget
			divide = 100
			multiply = 6
			add = KOR_nuclear_monthly_speed_bonus
		}
	}
	set_variable = {
		KOR_army_nuclear_factory_cost = {
			value = KOR_set_nuclear_budget
			multiply = 2
			round = yes
		}
	}
	set_variable = {
		KOR_army_nuclear_cost = {
			value = KOR_set_nuclear_budget
			divide = 100
			multiply = -3
		}
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

KOR_army_change_monthly_speed = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_nuclear_monthly_speed = KOR_army_nuke_speed_temp } # use this in effects
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_army_nuke_speed_temp < 0 }
		}
		multiply_variable = { KOR_army_nuke_speed_temp = -1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = SACW_monthly_speed_decrease_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = SACW_monthly_speed_increase_tt
	}
}


KOR_documents_clear_effect = {
	clear_array = KOR_top_secret_documents
}

KOR_bukhagui_recompute_tech_gap_modifiers = {
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var min = 0 max = 100 }

	set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_research_speed_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0 }
	PRC = {
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_research_speed_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var > 49 }
		}
		PRC = {
			set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_research_speed_factor_var = 0.12 }
			set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0.12 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var > 39 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var < 50 }
		}
		PRC = {
			set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_research_speed_factor_var = 0.08 }
			set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0.08 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var > 29 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var < 40 }
		}
		PRC = {
			set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_research_speed_factor_var = 0.04 }
			set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_prc_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0.04 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var > 19 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var < 30 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_research_speed_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var > 9 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_tech_gap_var < 20 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_research_speed_factor_var = 0.04 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0.04 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_research_speed_factor_var = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bukhagui_korea_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0.08 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = KOR_bukhagui_korea_tech_gap_dynamic
				}
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_bukhagui_korea_tech_gap_dynamic }
	}
	PRC = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = KOR_bukhagui_prc_tech_gap_dynamic
					}
				}
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_bukhagui_prc_tech_gap_dynamic }
		}
	}
}

KOR_nis_dismantlement_update_total_support_effect = {
	set_variable = {
		var = KOR_nis_dismantlement_total_support_var
		value = KOR_nis_dismantlement_public_support_var
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = KOR_nis_dismantlement_total_support_var
		value = KOR_nis_dismantlement_parliament_support_var
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_nis_dismantlement_total_support_var min = 0 max = 200 }
	set_variable = {
		var = KOR_nis_dismantlement_gui_var
		value = KOR_nis_dismantlement_total_support_var
	}
	divide_variable = { KOR_nis_dismantlement_gui_var = 2 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_nis_dismantlement_gui_var min = 0 max = 100 }
}

KMS_ECON_add_value_to_line = {

	add_to_variable = { KMS_ECON_count = 1 }
	set_variable = { KMS_ECON_last_y_position = KMS_ECON_y_position }

	set_variable = { KMS_ECON_y_position = KOR_economic_growth_rate_var }
	clamp_variable = { var = KMS_ECON_y_position min = 0 max = 10 }
	multiply_variable = { KMS_ECON_y_position = 5 }
	round_variable = KMS_ECON_y_position

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KMS_ECON_count > 20 }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = KMS_ECON_graph_offsets index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = KMS_ECON_graph_slopes index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = KMS_ECON_numbers index = 0 }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { offset = KMS_ECON_last_y_position }
	multiply_temp_variable = { offset = -1 }
	add_to_array = { KMS_ECON_graph_offsets = offset }

	set_temp_variable = { diff = KMS_ECON_y_position }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { diff = KMS_ECON_last_y_position }
	divide_temp_variable = { diff = 2 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { diff = 50 }
	add_to_array = { KMS_ECON_graph_slopes = diff }

	add_to_array = { KMS_ECON_numbers = KMS_ECON_count }
}

}

KOR_human_modification_initialize_dynamic_modifier_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_human_modification_dynamic } } }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_stability_factor_var = -0.10 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_minimum_training_level_var = 0.15 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_training_time_army_factor_var = 0.20 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_coordination_bonus_var = 0.20 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_special_forces_cap_var = 0.25 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_battalion_upkeep_factor_var = 0.25 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_aircraft_upkeep_factor_var = 0.25 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_ship_upkeep_factor_var = 0.25 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_military_development_monthly_var = 0.02 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_political_power_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_war_support_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_planning_speed_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_org_factor_var = 0.20 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_morale_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_experience_gain_army_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_attack_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_defence_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_breakthrough_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_speed_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_land_night_attack_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_dig_in_speed_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_max_dig_in_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_human_modification_consumer_goods_factor_var = 0 }
	}

	# 구버전 저장에서 이미 획득한 국민정신을 새 모디파이어로 이전합니다.
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_anticommunist_education_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_anticommunist_education_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_war_support_factor_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_planning_speed_var = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_combat_stress_recovery_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_combat_stress_recovery_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_morale_factor_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_experience_gain_army_factor_var = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_ideological_officers_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_ideological_officers_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_org_factor_var = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_political_power_factor_var = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_attack_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_attack_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_attack_factor_var = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_breakthrough_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_breakthrough_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_breakthrough_factor_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_planning_speed_var = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_close_quarters_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_close_quarters_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_army_defence_factor_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_dig_in_speed_factor_var = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_military_industry_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_military_industry_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor_var = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_total_war_production_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_total_war_production_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_consumer_goods_factor_var = 0.01 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_human_modification_propaganda_state_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_human_modification_propaganda_state_idea
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_stability_factor_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_war_support_factor_var = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_human_modification_political_power_factor_var = 0.05 }
	}
	KOR_human_modification_refresh_dynamic_modifier_effect = yes
}

KOR_riot_suppression_check_completion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_riot_suppressed_count_var > 16 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_riot_suppression_end_event_fired_flag }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_riot_suppression_end_event_fired_flag
		country_event = { id = korea.1181 }
	}
}

KOR_riot_suppress_busan_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_riot_resistance_busan_var = -5 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_riot_resistance_busan_var < 1 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_riot_resistance_busan_var = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_riot_suppression_busan_completed_flag } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_riot_suppressed_count_var = 1 }
			set_country_flag = KOR_riot_suppression_busan_completed_flag
			country_event = { id = korea.1201 hours = 6 }
		}
	}
	KOR_riot_suppression_check_completion_effect = yes
}

	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_ssn_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		#set_technology = {
		#	electric_torpedo = 1
		#}
		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "Yi Bongchang class"
			type = ship_hull_nuclear_submarine_2
			name_group = KOR_SSBN_HISTORICAL
			parent_version = 0
			allow_without_tech = yes
			modules = {
				fixed_ship_radar_slot = ship_sonar_2
				fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_mrbms_sub
				fixed_ship_engine_slot = sub_ship_nuclear_engine_1
				front_1_custom_slot = ship_torpedo_sub_2
				rear_1_custom_slot = empty
			}
		}

		add_equipment_production = {
			equipment = {
				type = ship_hull_nuclear_submarine_2
				creator = "KOR"
				version_name = "Yi Bongchang class"

		add_equipment_production = {
			equipment = {
				type = ship_hull_nuclear_submarine_2

### Behind the Curtain ###
KOR_BtC_initialize_political_war = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_BtC_initialize_political_war_tooltip
    clear_array = KOR_BtC_influence

    set_variable = { KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction = 0 }
    set_variable = { KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction = 1 }
    set_variable = { KOR_BtC_yushin_faction = 2 }

    add_to_array = { KOR_BtC_influence = 50 }  # mainstream 주류
    add_to_array = { KOR_BtC_influence = 30 }  # nonmainstream 비주류
    add_to_array = { KOR_BtC_influence = 20 }  # yushin 유신

	hidden_effect = {
		KOR = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_behind_the_curtain_buff_dynamic } }
	}

    KOR_BtC_recompute_political_war = yes
}

KOR_BtC_finish_political_war = {
    if = {
		limit = {
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = -1 }
		}
		country_event = { id = korea.261 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
		}
		country_event = { id = korea.261 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
		}
		country_event = { id = korea.276 }
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { id = korea.277 }
	}
}

KOR_BtC_recompute_political_war = {
    set_temp_variable = { total = 0 }
    for_each_loop = {
        array = KOR_BtC_influence
        value = influence_amnt

        add_to_temp_variable = { total = influence_amnt }
    }
    for_each_loop = {
        array = KOR_BtC_influence
        index = idx

        multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^idx = 100 }
        divide_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^idx = total }
    }

	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_mainstream_influence_display = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_influence_display = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_yushin_influence_display = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }

	KOR_BtC_redraw_icons = yes
}

KOR_BtC_redraw_icons = {
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence_x = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence_x = 3 }

	set_temp_variable = { tmp_x_yushin = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
	multiply_temp_variable = { tmp_x_yushin = 1.47 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence_x = tmp_x_yushin }

	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence_y = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence_y = 2.5 }

	set_temp_variable = { tmp_y_base = 262 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { tmp_y_base = KOR_BtC_influence_y }
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence_y = tmp_y_base }

	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction > KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction > KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction > KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction > KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction > KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
		    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction > KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
	}

	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_war_support_factor = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_army_attack_factor = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_industrial_capacity_factory = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
	if = {
	    limit = {
			NOT = {
			    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }
			}
	    }
	    if = {
		    limit = {
			   check_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
		    }
		    set_variable = { KOR_BtC_stability_factor = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction }
	    }
	    else_if = {
		    limit = {
			    check_variable = { KOR_BtC_winning_faction = KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
		    }
		    set_variable = { KOR_BtC_stability_factor = KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction }
	    }
	    else = {
		    set_variable = { KOR_BtC_stability_factor = 0 }
	    }
	}

	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_war_support_factor = 0.01 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_army_attack_factor = 0.01 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_industrial_capacity_factory = 0.01 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_stability_factor = -0.01 }

	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_war_support_factor = 0.25 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_army_attack_factor = 0.15 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_industrial_capacity_factory = 0.2 }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_stability_factor = 0.1 }
}

KOR_BtC_increase_influence_mainstream = {
	log = "Adding [?tmp_amount] influence to mainstream faction"

	set_variable = { KOR.amount = tmp_amount }
	set_variable = { KOR.faction = KOR.KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction }

    add_to_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^faction = amount }
    clamp_variable = { var = KOR_BtC_influence^faction min = 0 max = 100 }

    set_temp_variable = { total = 100 }
    subtract_from_temp_variable = { total = KOR_BtC_influence^faction }

    for_each_loop = {
        array = KOR_BtC_influence
        index = idx

        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { faction = idx } } }
            set_temp_variable = { to_subtract = amount }
            multiply_temp_variable = { to_subtract = KOR_BtC_influence^idx }
            divide_temp_variable = { to_subtract = total }

            subtract_from_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^idx = to_subtract }
            clamp_variable = { var = KOR_BtC_influence^idx min = 0 max = 100 }
        }
    }
    KOR_BtC_recompute_political_war = yes

}


KOR_BtC_increase_influence_nonmainstream = {
	log = "Adding [?tmp_amount] influence to nonmainstream faction"

	set_variable = { KOR.amount = tmp_amount }

        array = KOR_BtC_influence

KOR_BtC_randomize_influence = { #랜덤 영향력
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction = random }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_mainstream_faction = 100 }

	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction = random }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction = 100 }

	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction = random }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction = 100 }

	KOR_BtC_recompute_political_war = yes
}

	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_nonmainstream_faction = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_BtC_influence^KOR_BtC_yushin_faction = 100 }

	KOR_BtC_recompute_political_war = yes
}

KOR_lee_govenment_stability_var_clamp = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_lee_government_stability_var
		min = 0
		max = KOR_lee_government_stability_max_var
	}
}
KOR_DPK_cycle_pre_lee_var_clamp = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_lee_dpk_pre_lee_var
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
}
KOR_DPK_cycle_anti_lee_var_clamp = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_lee_dpk_anti_lee_var
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
}
KOR_DPK_cycle_anxious_var_clamp = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_lee_dpk_anxious_var
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
}
KOR_DPK_cycle_clamp = {
	KOR_DPK_cycle_pre_lee_var_clamp = yes
	KOR_DPK_cycle_anti_lee_var_clamp = yes
	KOR_DPK_cycle_anxious_var_clamp = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {	check_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 1 } }
			check_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_pre_lee_var = 100 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 2 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anti_lee_var = 100 } }
					check_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 3 }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 1 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_pre_lee_var = 100 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anti_lee_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anxious_var = 0 }
		add_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_pre_lee_idea
		remove_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_anti_lee_idea
		remove_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_anxious_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {	check_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 2 } }
			check_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anti_lee_var = 100 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 1 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_pre_lee_var = 100 } }
					check_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 3 }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_pre_lee_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anti_lee_var = 100 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anxious_var = 0 }
		remove_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_pre_lee_idea
		add_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_anti_lee_idea
		remove_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_anxious_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {	check_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 3 } }
			check_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anxious_var = 100 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_DPK_cycle_var = 3 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_pre_lee_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anti_lee_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_dpk_anxious_var = 100 }
		remove_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_pre_lee_idea
		remove_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_anti_lee_idea
		add_ideas = KOR_dpk_lead_anxious_idea
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = KOR_lee_reform_completion_var
				value = 100
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_lee_establishment_resistance_var = 0 }
	}
}

KOR_LJS_government_recalculate_value_label_positions = {
	clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_value_label_y
	for_each_loop = {
		array = LJS_government_positive_graph_values
		value = LJS_government_value_label_value
		set_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 84 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_value < 50 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 20 }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = LJS_government_value_label_value }
		add_to_array = { LJS_government_positive_graph_value_label_y = LJS_government_value_label_y }
	}

	clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_value_label_y
	for_each_loop = {
		array = LJS_government_negative_graph_values
		value = LJS_government_value_label_value
		set_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 84 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_value < 50 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 20 }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = LJS_government_value_label_value }
		add_to_array = { LJS_government_negative_graph_value_label_y = LJS_government_value_label_y }
	}
}

# 저장된 상세 수치 배열이 선 세그먼트 배열과 어긋나지 않도록,
# 선의 시작점과 기울기에서 각 세그먼트의 끝점 수치를 다시 만듭니다.
KOR_LJS_government_rebuild_value_labels_from_segments = {
	clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_values
	clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_values

	for_each_loop = {
		array = LJS_government_positive_graph_offsets
		index = LJS_government_value_label_idx
		set_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = LJS_government_positive_graph_slopes^LJS_government_value_label_idx }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = 50 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = 2 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = LJS_government_positive_graph_offsets^LJS_government_value_label_idx }
		round_temp_variable = LJS_government_reconstructed_value
		add_to_array = { LJS_government_positive_graph_values = LJS_government_reconstructed_value }
	}

	for_each_loop = {
		array = LJS_government_negative_graph_offsets
		index = LJS_government_value_label_idx
		set_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = LJS_government_negative_graph_slopes^LJS_government_value_label_idx }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = 50 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = 2 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_reconstructed_value = LJS_government_negative_graph_offsets^LJS_government_value_label_idx }
		round_temp_variable = LJS_government_reconstructed_value
		add_to_array = { LJS_government_negative_graph_values = LJS_government_reconstructed_value }
	}

	KOR_LJS_government_recalculate_value_label_positions = yes
	set_country_flag = LJS_government_graph_value_labels_initialized_flag
}

KOR_LJS_government_add_value_to_line = {

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { LJS_government_count > 0 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { LJS_government_graph_size > 0 } }
		}
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_offsets
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_slopes
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_values
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_value_label_y
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_offsets
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_slopes
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_values
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_value_label_y
		clear_array = LJS_government_numbers
		set_variable = { LJS_government_count = 0 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = LJS_government_graph_full_scale_flag }
		}
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_offsets
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_slopes
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_values
		clear_array = LJS_government_positive_graph_value_label_y
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_offsets
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_slopes
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_values
		clear_array = LJS_government_negative_graph_value_label_y
		clear_array = LJS_government_numbers
		set_variable = { LJS_government_count = 0 }
		set_variable = { LJS_government_graph_size = 0 }
		set_country_flag = LJS_government_graph_full_scale_flag
	}

	KOR_LJS_government_rebuild_value_labels_from_segments = yes

	add_to_variable = { LJS_government_count = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { LJS_government_graph_size = 1 }
	set_variable = { LJS_government_positive_last_y_position = LJS_government_positive_y_position }
	set_variable = { LJS_government_negative_last_y_position = LJS_government_negative_y_position }

	set_variable = { LJS_government_positive_y_position = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_visible }
	clamp_variable = { var = LJS_government_positive_y_position min = 0 max = 100 }
	round_variable = LJS_government_positive_y_position

	set_variable = { LJS_government_negative_y_position = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_bad_var }
	clamp_variable = { var = LJS_government_negative_y_position min = 0 max = 100 }
	round_variable = LJS_government_negative_y_position

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { LJS_government_count = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { LJS_government_positive_last_y_position = LJS_government_positive_y_position }
		set_variable = { LJS_government_negative_last_y_position = LJS_government_negative_y_position }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { LJS_government_graph_size > 20 }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_positive_graph_offsets index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_positive_graph_slopes index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_positive_graph_values index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_positive_graph_value_label_y index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_negative_graph_offsets index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_negative_graph_slopes index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_negative_graph_values index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_negative_graph_value_label_y index = 0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = LJS_government_numbers index = 0 }
		set_variable = { LJS_government_graph_size = 20 }
	}

	set_variable = { LJS_government_graph_x = 20 }
	subtract_from_variable = { LJS_government_graph_x = LJS_government_graph_size }
	multiply_variable = { LJS_government_graph_x = 20 }
	add_to_variable = { LJS_government_graph_x = 82 }

	set_temp_variable = { offset = LJS_government_positive_last_y_position }
	multiply_temp_variable = { offset = -1 }
	add_to_array = { LJS_government_positive_graph_offsets = offset }

	set_temp_variable = { diff = LJS_government_positive_y_position }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { diff = LJS_government_positive_last_y_position }
	divide_temp_variable = { diff = 2 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { diff = 50 }
	add_to_array = { LJS_government_positive_graph_slopes = diff }

	set_temp_variable = { offset = LJS_government_negative_last_y_position }
	multiply_temp_variable = { offset = -1 }
	add_to_array = { LJS_government_negative_graph_offsets = offset }

	set_temp_variable = { diff = LJS_government_negative_y_position }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { diff = LJS_government_negative_last_y_position }
	divide_temp_variable = { diff = 2 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { diff = 50 }
	add_to_array = { LJS_government_negative_graph_slopes = diff }

	add_to_array = { LJS_government_positive_graph_values = LJS_government_positive_y_position }
	set_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 84 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { LJS_government_positive_y_position < 50 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 20 }
	}
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = LJS_government_positive_y_position }
	add_to_array = { LJS_government_positive_graph_value_label_y = LJS_government_value_label_y }

	add_to_array = { LJS_government_negative_graph_values = LJS_government_negative_y_position }
	set_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 84 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { LJS_government_negative_y_position < 50 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = 20 }
	}
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { LJS_government_value_label_y = LJS_government_negative_y_position }
	add_to_array = { LJS_government_negative_graph_value_label_y = LJS_government_value_label_y }

	add_to_array = { LJS_government_numbers = LJS_government_count }
}

KOR_LJS_government_random_approval_rating_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var > 0 } }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var = 45 }
		set_variable = { KOR_LJS_approval_rating_neutral_var = 8 }
	}
	set_variable_to_random = {
		var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_change_temp
		min = -4
		max = 4
	}
	set_variable_to_random = {
		var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_neutral_change_temp
		min = -1.5
		max = 1.5
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
		value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_change_temp
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_neutral_var
		value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_neutral_change_temp
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var min = 5 max = 100 }
	KOR_LJS_clamp_approval_rating_to_cap_effect = yes
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_neutral_var min = 0 max = 15 }
	set_variable = {
		var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_bad_var
		value = 100
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_bad_var
		value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_bad_var
		value = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_neutral_var
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_bad_var min = 0 max = 100 }
}

TFRKP_reform_init_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_reform_tab = 1 }              # 기본 탭: 정치개혁
	set_variable = { KOR_kim_influence = 50 }          # 친-김민석파 영향력 (0~100)
	set_variable = { KOR_jeong_influence = 50 }        # 친-정청래파 영향력 (0~100)
	TFRKP_reform_update_influence_effect = yes
	TFRKP_reform_update_tab_frames_effect = yes
}

# 영향력 증감용 공용 효과
# 사용 예
#	set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_kim_influence_change = 10 }
#	TFRKP_reform_add_kim_influence_effect = yes
# (김민석파 +10, 정청래파 -10. 음수를 넣으면 반대로 작동)
TFRKP_reform_add_kim_influence_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_kim_influence = TFRKP_kim_influence_change }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_kim_influence min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { KOR_jeong_influence = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { KOR_jeong_influence = KOR_kim_influence }
	TFRKP_reform_update_influence_effect = yes
}

# 프로그레스바 프레임 재계산
# frame = (김민석파 영향력 / 5) 반올림 + 1  ->  1 ~ 21
TFRKP_reform_update_influence_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_influence_bar_frame = KOR_kim_influence }
	divide_variable = { KOR_influence_bar_frame = 5 }
	round_variable = KOR_influence_bar_frame
	add_to_variable = { KOR_influence_bar_frame = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_influence_bar_frame min = 1 max = 21 }
}

# 탭 버튼 하이라이트 프레임 갱신 (선택된 탭 = frame 2, 나머지 = frame 1)
TFRKP_reform_update_tab_frames_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_reform_tab_pol_frame = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_reform_tab_soc_frame = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_reform_tab_eco_frame = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_reform_tab = 1 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_reform_tab_pol_frame = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_reform_tab = 2 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_reform_tab_soc_frame = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_reform_tab = 3 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_reform_tab_eco_frame = 2 }
	}
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_initialize_effect = {
	set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_program_visible_flag
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_initialized_flag } }
		set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_initialized_flag
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var = 1 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_expected_days_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_factories_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_isolation_facility_level_var = 1 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_research_staff_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var = 30 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_cooldown_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_remaining_days_var = 100 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_research_speed_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_pending_symptom_count_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_confirmed_symptom_count_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_stockpile_var = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_configuration_confirmed_flag
		clr_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_in_progress_flag
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = KOR_secretly_great_military_network_idea }
		remove_ideas = KOR_secretly_great_military_network_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_secretly_great_military_network_dynamic } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_secretly_great_military_network_dynamic }
	}
	KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_metrics_effect = yes
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_start_bioweapon_production_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_completed_flag
			has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_configuration_confirmed_flag
			check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_confirmed_symptom_count_var = 3 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_in_progress_flag }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_in_progress_flag
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_dynamic } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_dynamic }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_dynamic
			days = 120
		}
		country_event = { id = TFRKP_hidden.27 days = 120 }
	}
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_complete_bioweapon_production_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_in_progress_flag }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_stockpile_var = 1 }
		clr_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_in_progress_flag
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_dynamic } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_special_strategic_materials_bioweapon_production_dynamic }
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_select_biochemical_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_route_selected_flag }
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 4
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_route_selected_flag
		set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_biochemical_route_flag
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_factories_var = 5 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_cooldown_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_research_speed_factor_var = 0.02 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_special_strategic_materials_factory_commitment_dynamic }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
		KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_display_effect = yes
		KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_metrics_effect = yes
	}
}

		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
		KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_display_effect = yes
		KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_metrics_effect = yes
	}
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_open_variant_configuration_effect = {
	set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_variant_configuration_open_flag
	set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_pending_symptom_count_var = 0 }
	clr_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_pending_symptom_cough_flag
	clr_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_pending_symptom_vomiting_flag

KOR_special_strategic_materials_append_keypad_digit_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_digit_count_var < 4 } }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_input_var = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_input_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_pressed_digit_var }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_digit_count_var = 1 }
		clr_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_access_denied_flag
	}
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_submit_keypad_effect = {
	# Access code: 6974
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_digit_count_var = 4 }
			check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_input_var = 6974 }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_group_four_keypad_unlocked_flag
		clr_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_open_flag
		KOR_special_strategic_materials_reset_keypad_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		KOR_special_strategic_materials_reset_keypad_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_keypad_access_denied_flag
	}
}
