# Call this effect from the target country's scope. It replaces any previous
# Korean payload on that country with the three symptoms currently confirmed by KOR.
KOR_apply_configured_bioweapon_payload_effect = {
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_cough_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_vomiting_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_immunosuppression_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_inflammation_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_systemic_infection_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_internal_bleeding_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_necrosis_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_hemorrhagic_shock_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_adrenal_fatigue_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_sensory_paralysis_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_paranoia_idea
	remove_ideas = KOR_bioweapon_symptom_hypersensitivity_idea

	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_configured_bioweapon_attack_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_configured_bioweapon_attack_dynamic }
	}

	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_org_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_morale_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_supply_consumption_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_attrition_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_mobilization_speed_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_land_reinforce_rate_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_stability_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_defence_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_production_speed_buildings_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_political_power_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_planning_speed_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_war_support_factor_var = 0 }

	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_cough_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_org_factor_var = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_morale_factor_var = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_vomiting_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_supply_consumption_factor_var = 0.04 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_attrition_var = 0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_immunosuppression_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = -0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_mobilization_speed_var = -0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_inflammation_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_morale_factor_var = -0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_land_reinforce_rate_var = -0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_systemic_infection_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_stability_factor_var = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_industrial_capacity_factory_var = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_internal_bleeding_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_org_factor_var = -0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_defence_factor_var = -0.04 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_necrosis_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_production_speed_buildings_factor_var = -0.04 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_hemorrhagic_shock_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_morale_factor_var = -0.08 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_org_factor_var = -0.04 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_adrenal_fatigue_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_political_power_factor_var = -0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor_var = -0.04 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_sensory_paralysis_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_planning_speed_var = -0.06 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_land_reinforce_rate_var = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_paranoia_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_stability_factor_var = -0.04 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_political_power_factor_var = -0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_hypersensitivity_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_war_support_factor_var = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_attrition_var = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_irritable_bowel_syndrome_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_supply_consumption_factor_var = -0.10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_sex_hormone_imbalance_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_mobilization_speed_var = -0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_alopecia_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_stability_factor_var = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_war_support_factor_var = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_sexual_dysfunction_flag } }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = -0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_political_power_factor_var = -0.03 }
	}

	# The corona-variant route amplifies the configured payload without changing
	# the original biological-weapon values above.
	if = {
		limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_corona_variant_route_flag } }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_org_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_morale_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_supply_consumption_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_attrition_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_mobilization_speed_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_land_reinforce_rate_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_stability_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_defence_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_production_speed_buildings_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_political_power_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_planning_speed_var = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_war_support_factor_var = 1.5 }

		# Group IV represents postmortem reactivation and is deliberately more severe.
		if = {
			limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_irritable_bowel_syndrome_flag } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_supply_consumption_factor_var = 0.35 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_attrition_var = 0.06 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_sex_hormone_imbalance_flag } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = -0.015 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_mobilization_speed_var = -0.025 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_planning_speed_var = -0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_alopecia_flag } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_stability_factor_var = -0.015 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_war_support_factor_var = -0.015 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_attrition_var = 0.04 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { KOR = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_symptom_sexual_dysfunction_flag } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_recruitable_population_factor_var = -0.01 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_political_power_factor_var = -0.015 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_configured_bioweapon_army_morale_factor_var = -0.08 }
		}
	}

	add_dynamic_modifier = {
		modifier = KOR_configured_bioweapon_attack_dynamic
		days = 120
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_metrics_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_research_staff_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_factories_var }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_research_staff_var = 68 }
	round_variable = KOR_special_strategic_materials_research_staff_var

	set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var = 35 }
	set_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_risk_temp = KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var }
	add_to_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_risk_temp = -1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_risk_temp = 4 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_risk_temp }

	set_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_factory_risk_temp = KOR_special_strategic_materials_factories_var }
	add_to_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_factory_risk_temp = -5 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_factory_risk_temp > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_factory_risk_temp = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_factory_risk_temp }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_isolation_risk_temp = KOR_special_strategic_materials_isolation_facility_level_var }
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_isolation_risk_temp = -5 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_isolation_risk_temp }
	round_variable = KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var min = 5 max = 75 }

	set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_remaining_days_var = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_remaining_days_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_cooldown_var }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_remaining_days_var min = 0 max = 100 }

	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_display_effect = {
	set_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp = KOR_special_strategic_materials_factories_var }
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp = 0.5 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp = 1 }

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_completed_flag }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var = 5 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = 100 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_expected_days_var = 0 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_expected_days_var = 2500 }
		subtract_from_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_expected_days_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var }
		divide_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_expected_days_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp }
		round_variable = KOR_special_strategic_materials_expected_days_var
		clamp_variable = { var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_expected_days_var min = 0 max = 2500 }

		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var < 500 } }
			set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var < 1000 } }
			set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var = 2 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = -500 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var < 1500 } }
			set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var = 3 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = -1000 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var < 2000 } }
			set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var = 4 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = -1500 }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_var = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = -2000 }
		}
		divide_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var = 5 }
		round_variable = KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var
		clamp_variable = { var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_stage_progress_display_var min = 0 max = 100 }
	}
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_check_completion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var > 2499 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_completed_flag }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_completed_flag
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_biochemical_route_flag }
			set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_biochemical_developed_flag
			set_country_flag = KOR_bio_weapon
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_corona_variant_route_flag }
			set_country_flag = KOR_special_strategic_materials_corona_variant_developed_flag
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_special_strategic_materials_factory_commitment_dynamic }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_factories_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_research_speed_factor_var = 0 }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_daily_progress_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_cooldown_var = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_cooldown_var min = 0 max = 100 }

	set_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp = KOR_special_strategic_materials_factories_var }
	multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp = 0.5 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_speed_temp }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_special_strategic_materials_progress_var min = 0 max = 2500 }
	KOR_special_strategic_materials_check_completion_effect = yes
	KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_display_effect = yes
	KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_metrics_effect = yes
}

KOR_special_strategic_materials_weaponization_test_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_test_cooldown_var = 0 }
	KOR_special_strategic_materials_update_metrics_effect = yes

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var < 11 } }
		random_list = {
			90 = { country_event = { id = korea.1252 hours = 1 } }
			10 = { country_event = { id = korea.1251 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var < 21 } }
		random_list = {
			80 = { country_event = { id = korea.1252 hours = 1 } }
			20 = { country_event = { id = korea.1251 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var < 31 } }
		random_list = {
			70 = { country_event = { id = korea.1252 hours = 1 } }
			30 = { country_event = { id = korea.1251 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var < 41 } }
		random_list = {
			60 = { country_event = { id = korea.1252 hours = 1 } }
			40 = { country_event = { id = korea.1251 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var < 51 } }
		random_list = {
			50 = { country_event = { id = korea.1252 hours = 1 } }
			50 = { country_event = { id = korea.1251 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_special_strategic_materials_accident_risk_var < 61 } }
		random_list = {
			40 = { country_event = { id = korea.1252 hours = 1 } }
			60 = { country_event = { id = korea.1251 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			30 = { country_event = { id = korea.1252 hours = 1 } }
			70 = { country_event = { id = korea.1251 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

				desc = KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char_army_fail_desc
				traits = { kor_yoon_tomorrow_president_trait }
				ideology = korean_social_democracy
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
	}
	KOR_lee_jun_seok_char = {
		promote_character = korean_conservatism
	}
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = social_liberal
				last_election = "[CURRENT_DATE]"
				election_frequency = 60
				elections_allowed = yes
			}
		}
		CURRENT_DATE = "[?global.date.GetDateStringNoHour]"
	}
	end_coalition = yes
	set_temp_variable = { coalition_partner_var_temp = token:social_democrat }
	add_to_coalition = yes
	set_temp_variable = { coalition_partner_var_temp = token:market_liberal }
	add_to_coalition = yes
	set_temp_variable = { coalition_partner_var_temp = token:conservative }
	add_to_coalition = yes
	set_party_name = {
		ideology = authoritarian_democrat
		long_name = KOR_party_being_liquidated_long
		name = KOR_party_being_liquidated
	}
	set_party_name = {
		ideology = nationalist
		long_name = KOR_party_being_liquidated_long
		name = KOR_party_being_liquidated
	}
	set_party_name = {
		ideology = communist
		long_name = KOR_communist_party_DLP_long
		name = KOR_communist_party_DLP
	}
	set_party_name = {
		ideology = libertarian_socialist
		long_name = KOR_libertarian_socialist_party_DLP_long
		name = KOR_libertarian_socialist_party_DLP
	}
	set_country_flag = KOR_army_nam_route_happy_end_clear_flag
}

KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_break_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_flag
			check_variable = { KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_stack_var > 0 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_stack_var = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_stack_var < 1 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_stack_var = 0 }
			clr_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_flag
			set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_broken_flag
			country_event = { id = korea.1361 hours = 6 }
		}
	}
}

KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_collapse_penalty_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_collapse_penalty_applied_flag }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_collapse_penalty_applied_flag

			ideology = social_liberal
			popularity = -0.03

			ideology = conservative
			popularity = -0.03
		}

			var = KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var
			value = -8
		}
		KOR_LJS_clamp_approval_rating_to_cap_effect = yes
	}
}

# 컨텐츠 시작 시 초기화 (이벤트 kimminseok_korea_war.1)
kimminseok_korea_war_init = {
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_tension = 10 }
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_discontent = 10 }
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_deadline_days = 60 }
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_prog_warprep = 0 }
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_prog_unrest = 0 }
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_prog_asym = 0 }
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_assim = 0 }
	set_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_eastsea_open = 0 }
	kimminseok_korea_war_update_ui = yes
}

# 값 클램프 + 바 프레임(1~11) + dirty 갱신
kimminseok_korea_war_update_ui = {
	clamp_variable = { var = kimminseok_korea_war_tension min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = kimminseok_korea_war_discontent min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = kimminseok_korea_war_assim min = 0 max = 100 }

	add_to_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_dirty = 1 }
}

# 9개 준비 디시전 완주 검사 (각 디시전 remove_effect 에서 호출)
kimminseok_korea_war_check_all_done = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			kimminseok_korea_war_all_prep_done_trigger = yes
			has_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_countdown_active
		}
		clr_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_countdown_active
		set_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_prep_complete
		country_event = { id = kimminseok_korea_war.20 }
	}
}


# TFR Korea Plus - 국방개혁 의제 시스템 scripted effects
#
# ── 사용법 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# 1) 시스템 시작 시 1회: TFRKP_defreform_init_effect = yes
# 2) 토큰 지급 (중점/이벤트 등):
#      set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_defreform_token_change = 1 }
#      TFRKP_defreform_add_token_effect = yes
# 3) 진척도/신뢰도 증감:
#      set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_defreform_progress_change = 10 }
#      TFRKP_defreform_add_progress_effect = yes
#      set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_defreform_trust_change = 5 }
#      TFRKP_defreform_add_trust_effect = yes
# 4) 세계 육군 순위 변동 (음수 = 순위 상승):
#      set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_defreform_rank_change = -1 }
#      TFRKP_defreform_add_rank_effect = yes
# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

TFRKP_defreform_init_effect = {
	set_country_flag = TFRKP_show_defreform_GUI	# 국방개혁 카테고리 표시 플래그
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_var = 3 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_progress_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_trust_var = 50 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_rank_var = 6 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_focus_area = 0 }
	TFRKP_defreform_update_poster_frames_effect = yes
	TFRKP_defreform_update_visuals_effect = yes
}

TFRKP_defreform_update_visuals_effect = {
	# 토큰 프레임 (보유 수 이하 = 2, 초과 = 1)
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_1 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_2 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_3 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_4 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_5 = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_var > 0 } } set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_1 = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_var > 1 } } set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_2 = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_var > 2 } } set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_3 = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_var > 3 } } set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_4 = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_var > 4 } } set_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_frame_5 = 2 } }

	# 진척도 프레임 (0~100 -> 1~21)
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_progress_frame = KOR_defreform_progress_var }
	divide_variable = { KOR_defreform_progress_frame = 5 }
	round_variable = KOR_defreform_progress_frame
	add_to_variable = { KOR_defreform_progress_frame = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_defreform_progress_frame min = 1 max = 21 }

	# 신뢰도 프레임
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_trust_frame = KOR_defreform_trust_var }
	divide_variable = { KOR_defreform_trust_frame = 5 }
	round_variable = KOR_defreform_trust_frame
	add_to_variable = { KOR_defreform_trust_frame = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_defreform_trust_frame min = 1 max = 21 }
}

TFRKP_defreform_add_token_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_defreform_token_var = TFRKP_defreform_token_change }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_defreform_token_var min = 0 max = 5 }
	TFRKP_defreform_update_visuals_effect = yes
	custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKP_defreform_add_tt
}

TFRKP_defreform_add_progress_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_defreform_progress_var = TFRKP_defreform_progress_change }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_defreform_progress_var min = 0 max = 100 }
	TFRKP_defreform_update_visuals_effect = yes
}

TFRKP_defreform_add_trust_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_defreform_trust_var = TFRKP_defreform_trust_change }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_defreform_trust_var min = 0 max = 100 }
	TFRKP_defreform_update_visuals_effect = yes
}

TFRKP_defreform_add_rank_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_defreform_rank_var = TFRKP_defreform_rank_change }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_defreform_rank_var min = 1 max = 30 }
}


# 포스터 하이라이트 프레임 갱신 (선택 = 2, 나머지 = 1)
TFRKP_defreform_update_poster_frames_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_poster_frame_1 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_poster_frame_2 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_defreform_poster_frame_3 = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_focus_area = 1 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_defreform_poster_frame_1 = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_focus_area = 2 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_defreform_poster_frame_2 = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_defreform_focus_area = 3 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_defreform_poster_frame_3 = 2 }
	}
}
# TFR Korea Plus - 이카루스의 비상 scripted effects
#
# ── 사용법 요약 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# 1) 시스템 시작 시(중점 완료 등) 1회:
#      TFRKP_icarus_init_effect = yes
#    (오픈 버튼 표시 플래그 TFRKP_show_icarus_GUI 도 함께 세팅됨)
#
# 2) 각 국면의 "주요 개혁" 디시전 remove_effect 에서:
#      TFRKP_icarus_stage_ready_effect = yes
#    -> 비상 버튼이 활성화되고, 플레이어가 버튼을 눌러 고도 +1
#
# 3) 밀랍(개혁 동력) 증감이 필요할 때:
#      set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_icarus_wax_change = 20 }
#      TFRKP_icarus_add_wax_effect = yes
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

TFRKP_kms_add_political_decision_effect = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKP_kms_add_political_decision_effect_tt
	add_to_variable = { KOR_kimminseok_reform_decision_click_7 = 1 }
}
TFRKP_kms_add_army_decision_effect = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKP_kms_add_army_decision_effect_tt
	add_to_variable = { KOR_kimminseok_military_decision_click_7 = 1 }
}

# 초기화
TFRKP_icarus_init_effect = {
	set_country_flag = TFRKP_show_icarus_GUI
	set_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_icarus_wax = 100 }
	TFRKP_icarus_update_visuals_effect = yes
}

# 시각 요소 갱신 (트랙 프레임 / 마커 y / 밀랍 프레임)
TFRKP_icarus_update_visuals_effect = {
	# 트랙 프레임 = 고도 + 1 (1~11)
	set_variable = { KOR_icarus_track_frame = KOR_icarus_altitude }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_icarus_track_frame = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_icarus_track_frame min = 1 max = 11 }

	# 마커 x = 31 + (고도 x 36)  (지상 31 -> 태양 앞 391)
	set_variable = { KOR_icarus_marker_x = KOR_icarus_altitude }
	multiply_variable = { KOR_icarus_marker_x = 36 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_icarus_marker_x = 31 }

	# 밀랍 프레임 = (밀랍 / 5) 반올림 + 1 (1~21)
	set_variable = { KOR_icarus_wax_frame = KOR_icarus_wax }
	divide_variable = { KOR_icarus_wax_frame = 5 }
	round_variable = KOR_icarus_wax_frame
	add_to_variable = { KOR_icarus_wax_frame = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_icarus_wax_frame min = 1 max = 21 }
}

# 밀랍 증감 (temp: TFRKP_icarus_wax_change)
TFRKP_icarus_add_wax_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_icarus_wax = TFRKP_icarus_wax_change }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_icarus_wax min = 0 max = 100 }
	TFRKP_icarus_update_visuals_effect = yes
}

# 주요 개혁 완료 -> 다음 비상 허가
TFRKP_icarus_stage_ready_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { TFRKP_icarus_stage_ready_var = 1 }
}

# 비상 (고도 +1, 밀랍 -10, 국민정신 교체)
TFRKP_icarus_ascend_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude < 10 }
			check_variable = { KOR_icarus_wax > 9 }
			check_variable = { TFRKP_icarus_stage_ready_var > 0 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { TFRKP_icarus_stage_ready_var = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { TFRKP_icarus_wax_change = -10 }
		TFRKP_icarus_add_wax_effect = yes

		# 국민정신 교체 (이전 단계 제거 -> 새 단계 부여)
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 1 } }
			add_ideas = KOR_icarus_flight_1
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 2 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_1 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 3 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_2 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 4 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_3 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_4 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 5 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_4 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 6 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_5 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_6 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 7 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_6 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_7 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 8 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_7 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_8 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 9 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_8 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_9 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_icarus_altitude = 10 } }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_9 add_idea = KOR_icarus_flight_10 }
			# 최고 고도 도달 - 기존 엔딩 트리거용 플래그
			set_country_flag = TFRKP_icarus_transcendence_flag
		}

		TFRKP_icarus_update_visuals_effect = yes
	}
}

# TFR Korea Plus - 총선/개헌 2페이즈 시스템 (순수 GUI, 디시전 미사용)
#
# [1페이즈] 2028 총선 유세
#   지역: 1 경기 / 2 강원 / 3 충청 / 4 호남 / 5 영남
#   유세: 1 유세 집중 / 2 조직 정비 / 3 공약 발표 (각 60일, 승리 포인트 +4)
#   의석 = 168 + 승리 포인트  (15회 전부 완수 시 228석, 개헌선 = 200석)
# [2페이즈] 제10차 개헌
#   쟁점 1 대통령 임기 / 2 국무총리 / 3 감사원 / 4 지방분권 (순서대로)
#   버튼1 = 개혁안 채택 / 버튼2 = 현행 유지 / 버튼3 = 보류
#
# Lifecycle driver: common/on_actions/00_TFRKP_on_actions.txt
# Initialization: focus reward, with an on_daily fallback for older saves
# Daily progress: TFRKP_regiondev_daily_tick_effect
# Election result: TFRKP_regiondev_election_resolve_effect
# Phase 2: TFRKP_constitution_start_effect
# Great Asian War lock: TFRKP_constitution_lock_effect

TFRKP_regiondev_init_effect = {
	set_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_enabled_flag
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_phase = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_selected = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_region = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_agenda = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_left = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_points = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_seats = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_constitution_issue = 1 }

	clear_array = KOR_regiondev_done_a1
	clear_array = KOR_regiondev_done_a2
	clear_array = KOR_regiondev_done_a3
	clear_array = KOR_constitution_choice
	for_loop_effect = {
		end = 6
		add_to_array = { KOR_regiondev_done_a1 = 0 }
		add_to_array = { KOR_regiondev_done_a2 = 0 }
		add_to_array = { KOR_regiondev_done_a3 = 0 }
	}
	for_loop_effect = {
		end = 5
		add_to_array = { KOR_constitution_choice = 0 }
	}
	TFRKP_regiondev_update_frames_effect = yes
}

TFRKP_regiondev_update_frames_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_1 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_2 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_3 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_4 = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_5 = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_selected = 1 } } set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_1 = 2 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_selected = 2 } } set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_2 = 2 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_selected = 3 } } set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_3 = 2 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_selected = 4 } } set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_4 = 2 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_selected = 5 } } set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_frame_5 = 2 } }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_region > 0 } }
		set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_progress_frame = 15 }
		subtract_from_variable = { KOR_regiondev_progress_frame = KOR_regiondev_days_left }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_regiondev_progress_frame = 20 }
		divide_variable = { KOR_regiondev_progress_frame = 15 }
		round_variable = KOR_regiondev_progress_frame
		add_to_variable = { KOR_regiondev_progress_frame = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = KOR_regiondev_progress_frame min = 1 max = 21 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_progress_frame = 1 }
	}
}

# 의제 버튼 클릭 (페이즈 분기)
TFRKP_regiondev_agenda_click_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_phase = 1 } }
		# 유세 시작 (60일)
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_region = 0 }
				check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_selected > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_region = KOR_regiondev_selected }
			set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_agenda = TFRKP_regiondev_agenda_pick }
			set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_left = 15 }
			TFRKP_regiondev_update_frames_effect = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_phase = 2 } }
		# 개헌 쟁점 선택 (1 채택 / 2 현행 유지 / 3 보류) 후 다음 쟁점으로
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_constitution_locked_flag }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_constitution_done_flag }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_constitution_choice^KOR_constitution_issue = TFRKP_regiondev_agenda_pick }
			add_to_variable = { KOR_constitution_issue = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { KOR_constitution_issue > 4 } }
				set_country_flag = TFRKP_constitution_done_flag
				set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_fake_var = 1000 }
				set_country_flag = KOR_const_countdown_active
				country_event = { id = kor_countdown.1 days = 1 }
				set_variable = { KOR_constitution_issue = 4 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# 유세 취소
TFRKP_regiondev_cancel_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_region = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_agenda = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_left = 0 }
	TFRKP_regiondev_update_frames_effect = yes
}

# 매일 틱 (1페이즈 유세만)
TFRKP_regiondev_daily_tick_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_phase = 1 }
			check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_region > 0 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_left = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_left < 1 } }
			# 유세 완료: 승리 포인트 +4, 완료 기록
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_regiondev_agenda_1_click_flag
				}
				add_to_variable = { KOR_regiondev_points = 3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_regiondev_agenda_2_click_flag
				}
				add_to_variable = { KOR_regiondev_points = 4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_regiondev_agenda_3_click_flag
				}
				add_to_variable = { KOR_regiondev_points = 5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_agenda = 1 } }
				set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_done_a1^KOR_regiondev_active_region = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_agenda = 2 } }
				set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_done_a2^KOR_regiondev_active_region = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_done_a3^KOR_regiondev_active_region = 1 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_region = 0 }
			set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_active_agenda = 0 }
			set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_left = 0 }
		}
		TFRKP_regiondev_update_frames_effect = yes
	}
}

TFRKP_2028_election_point_add = {
	add_to_variable = { KOR_regiondev_points = KOR_regiondev_points_temp_var }
	custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKP_2028_election_point_add_tt
}

# 2028 총선 정산 (선거 이벤트에서 호출)
# 의석 = 168 + 승리 포인트, 200석 이상 = 개헌선 확보
TFRKP_regiondev_election_resolve_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_seats = 168 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_regiondev_seats = KOR_regiondev_points }
	clamp_variable = { var = KOR_regiondev_seats min = 168 max = 228 }
	set_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_election_done_flag
	clr_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_supermajority_flag
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_seats > 199 } }
		set_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_supermajority_flag
		set_country_flag = KOR_lee_2028_election_flag
	}

	# Keep the actual 23rd Assembly composition in sync with the campaign result.
	set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = KOR_regiondev_seats }
	set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 297 }
	subtract_from_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = KOR_regiondev_seats }
	set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 1 }
	set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 2 }
	set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
	KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes

	TFRKP_regiondev_cancel_effect = yes
}

# 2페이즈 개시 (개헌 정국 이벤트에서 호출)
TFRKP_constitution_start_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_regiondev_phase = 2 }
	set_variable = { KOR_constitution_issue = 1 }
}

# 대아대전 발발 -> 개헌위원회 해산 (전쟁 이벤트에서 호출)
TFRKP_constitution_lock_effect = {
	set_country_flag = TFRKP_constitution_locked_flag
	clr_country_flag = KOR_const_countdown_active
	clr_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_enabled_flag
}

KOR_three_principles_initialize_democracy_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_three_principles_democracy_initialized_flag }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_three_principles_democracy_initialized_flag
		set_variable = { KOR_three_principles_democracy_value_var = 10 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_stability_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_political_power_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_intel_network_gain_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_intelligence_agency_defense_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_enemy_operative_capture_chance_factor_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_resistance_growth_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_industrial_capacity_factory_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_cho_guk_democracy_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor_var = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = KOR_cho_guk_democracy_dynamic
				}
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = KOR_cho_guk_democracy_dynamic
		}
	}
}

KOR_three_principles_refresh_democracy_dynamic_modifier_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = KOR_cho_guk_democracy_dynamic
			}
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = KOR_cho_guk_democracy_dynamic
		}
	}
	add_dynamic_modifier = {
		modifier = KOR_cho_guk_democracy_dynamic
	}
}

KOR_yec_initialize_space_revolution_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_yec_space_revolution_initialized_flag }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_yec_space_revolution_initialized_flag

		set_variable = { KOR_yec_space_page_var = 0 }
		set_country_flag = KOR_yec_default_page_initialized_flag
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_gui_dirty_var = 1 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_count_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var = 100 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_effectiveness_factor_var = 1 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_per_satellite_factor_var = -0.02 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_penalty_preview_var = 0 }

		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_var = 1 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_minus_one_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_factories_committed_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_total_days_var = 300 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var = 300 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = 0 }

		set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_required_minus_one_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_factories_committed_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_remaining_days_var = 0 }

		set_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_theatre_var = 0 }

		KOR_yec_refresh_production_selection_effect = yes
		KOR_yec_refresh_maintenance_requirement_effect = yes
		KOR_yec_update_satellite_status_effect = yes
	}
}

KOR_yec_refresh_production_selection_effect = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_var
		min = 1
		max = 10
	}

	set_variable = {
		KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_minus_one_var = KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_var
	}
	add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_minus_one_var = -1 }

	set_variable = {
		KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var = KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_var
	}
	multiply_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var = -30 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var = 330 }
	round_variable = KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var
		min = 30
		max = 300
	}
}

KOR_yec_start_satellite_production_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_yec_satellite_production_active_flag }
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > ROOT.KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_minus_one_var
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_yec_satellite_production_active_flag
		set_variable = {
			KOR_yec_satellite_factories_committed_var = KOR_yec_satellite_factories_selected_var
		}
		set_variable = {
			KOR_yec_satellite_production_total_days_var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var
		}
		set_variable = {
			KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_preview_days_var
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = 0 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_production_factory_commitment_dynamic
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}

KOR_yec_update_satellite_production_progress_effect = {
	set_variable = {
		KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_total_days_var
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var
	}
	multiply_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = 100 }
	divide_variable = {
		KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_total_days_var
	}
	round_variable = KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
}

KOR_yec_complete_satellite_production_effect = {
	clr_country_flag = KOR_yec_satellite_production_active_flag
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_production_factory_commitment_dynamic
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_production_factory_commitment_dynamic
		}
	}
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_factories_committed_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = 100 }

	add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_count_var = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var = -3 }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}

	KOR_yec_refresh_maintenance_requirement_effect = yes
	KOR_yec_update_satellite_status_effect = yes
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

KOR_yec_debug_prepare_emp_test_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_yec_space_revolution_initialized_flag
			}
		}
		KOR_yec_initialize_space_revolution_effect = yes
	}

	clr_country_flag = KOR_yec_satellite_production_active_flag
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_production_factory_commitment_dynamic
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_production_factory_commitment_dynamic
		}
	}
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_factories_committed_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_progress_var = 100 }

	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_count_var = 15 }
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var = 60 }
	KOR_yec_refresh_maintenance_requirement_effect = yes
	KOR_yec_update_satellite_status_effect = yes
	add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_gui_dirty_var = 1 }
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

KOR_yec_daily_satellite_production_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_yec_satellite_production_active_flag
		}
		add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var = -1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var
			min = 0
			max = 300
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_production_remaining_days_var < 1 }
			}
			KOR_yec_complete_satellite_production_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			KOR_yec_update_satellite_production_progress_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

KOR_yec_refresh_maintenance_requirement_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_count_var > 0 }
		}
		set_variable = {
			KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var = KOR_yec_satellite_count_var
		}
		add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var = 2 }
		divide_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var = 5 }
		round_variable = KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var
		clamp_variable = {
			var = KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var
			min = 1
			max = 999
		}
		set_variable = {
			KOR_yec_maintenance_required_minus_one_var = KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var
		}
		add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_required_minus_one_var = -1 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_required_minus_one_var = 0 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_yec_maintenance_active_flag
		}
		set_variable = {
			KOR_yec_maintenance_factories_committed_var = KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}

KOR_yec_start_maintenance_effect = {
	KOR_yec_refresh_maintenance_requirement_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_yec_maintenance_active_flag }
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_count_var > 0 }
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var < 100 }
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > ROOT.KOR_yec_maintenance_required_minus_one_var
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_yec_maintenance_active_flag
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_remaining_days_var = 180 }
		set_variable = {
			KOR_yec_maintenance_factories_committed_var = KOR_yec_maintenance_required_factories_var
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_maintenance_factory_commitment_dynamic
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}

KOR_yec_end_maintenance_effect = {
	clr_country_flag = KOR_yec_maintenance_active_flag
	remove_dynamic_modifier = {
		modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_maintenance_factory_commitment_dynamic
	}
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_factories_committed_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_remaining_days_var = 0 }
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

KOR_yec_daily_maintenance_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_yec_maintenance_active_flag
		}
		add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_remaining_days_var = -1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = KOR_yec_maintenance_remaining_days_var
			min = 0
			max = 180
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_yec_maintenance_remaining_days_var < 1 }
			}
			KOR_yec_end_maintenance_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

KOR_yec_clear_satellite_status_modifiers_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_optimal_dynamic }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_optimal_dynamic }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_normal_dynamic }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_normal_dynamic }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_degraded_dynamic }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_degraded_dynamic }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_critical_dynamic }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_critical_dynamic }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_disabled_dynamic }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_disabled_dynamic }
	}
}

KOR_yec_update_satellite_status_effect = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var > 89 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_effectiveness_factor_var = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_optimal_dynamic }
			}
			KOR_yec_clear_satellite_status_modifiers_effect = yes
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_optimal_dynamic
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var > 69 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_effectiveness_factor_var = 0.9 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_normal_dynamic }
			}
			KOR_yec_clear_satellite_status_modifiers_effect = yes
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_normal_dynamic
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var > 49 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_effectiveness_factor_var = 0.7 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_degraded_dynamic }
			}
			KOR_yec_clear_satellite_status_modifiers_effect = yes
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_degraded_dynamic
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var > 29 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_effectiveness_factor_var = 0.4 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_critical_dynamic }
			}
			KOR_yec_clear_satellite_status_modifiers_effect = yes
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_critical_dynamic
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_effectiveness_factor_var = 0.15 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_disabled_dynamic }
			}
			KOR_yec_clear_satellite_status_modifiers_effect = yes
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = KOR_yec_satellite_status_disabled_dynamic
			}
		}
	}

	set_variable = {
		KOR_yec_emp_penalty_preview_var = KOR_yec_satellite_count_var
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		KOR_yec_emp_penalty_preview_var = KOR_yec_emp_per_satellite_factor_var
	}
	set_temp_variable = {
		KOR_yec_emp_availability_factor_temp = KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var
	}
	divide_temp_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_availability_factor_temp = 100 }
	multiply_variable = {
		KOR_yec_emp_penalty_preview_var = KOR_yec_emp_availability_factor_temp
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

KOR_yec_monthly_availability_tick_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_count_var > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var = -3 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_yec_maintenance_active_flag
			}
			add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var = 4 }
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = KOR_yec_satellite_availability_var
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		KOR_yec_update_satellite_status_effect = yes
	}
}

KOR_yec_activate_selected_emp_blackout_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_theatre_var = 1 }
		}
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = KOR_yec_emp_west_blackout_flag
			days = 90
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_theatre_var = 2 }
		}
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = KOR_yec_emp_east_blackout_flag
			days = 90
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_theatre_var = 3 }
		}
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = KOR_yec_emp_north_blackout_flag
			days = 90
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { KOR_yec_gui_dirty_var = 1 }
}

KOR_yec_launch_civil_emp_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_emp_theatre_var > 0 }
			check_variable = { KOR_yec_satellite_count_var > 0 }
			KOR_yec_has_civil_emp_cost_trigger = yes
			KOR_yec_is_selected_emp_theatre_available_for_strike_trigger = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_yec_civil_emp_cooldown_flag
			}
			PRC = {
				exists = yes
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
			any_state = {
				KOR_yec_is_selected_emp_theatre_state_trigger = yes
				controller = {
					exists = yes
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = KOR_yec_civil_emp_cooldown_flag
			days = 90
			value = 1
		}
		KOR_yec_activate_selected_emp_blackout_effect = yes
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				KOR_yec_is_selected_emp_theatre_state_trigger = yes
				controller = {
					exists = yes
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				KOR_yec_emp_state_penalty_var = ROOT.KOR_yec_emp_penalty_preview_var
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = KOR_yec_civil_emp_state_dynamic
				days = 90
			}
		}
	}
}

KOR_bb_reset_outcount_effect = {
	set_variable = { KOR_baseball_outcount_var = 0 } # Outcount Reset
}
KOR_bb_add_outcount_effect = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_bb_add_outcount_effect_tt
	add_to_variable = { KOR_baseball_outcount_var = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_baseball_outcount_var = 3 }
		}
		KOR_bb_add_inning_effect = yes
	}
	KOR_bb_outcount_clamp = yes
	hidden_effect = {
		country_event = korea.854
	}
}
KOR_bb_add_inning_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { KOR_base_up_total_score_var > KOR_base_down_total_score_var_10 }
				check_variable = { KOR_base_down_total_score_var > KOR_base_up_total_score_var_10 }
			}	
			check_variable = { KOR_baseball_inning > 10 }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					KOR_baseball_inning > 17
				}
			}
		}
		KOR_bb_cold_game_effect = yes
		KOR_bb_end_game_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { KOR_base_up_total_score_var > KOR_base_down_total_score_var_10 }
				check_variable = { KOR_base_down_total_score_var > KOR_base_up_total_score_var_10 }
			}
			check_variable = {
				KOR_baseball_inning > 17
			}
		}
		KOR_bb_end_game_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { baseball_top_bottom_var = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { baseball_top_bottom_var = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { KOR_baseball_now_game_var = 1 } 
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_bb_add_inning_next_dpk_tt
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { baseball_top_bottom_var = 2 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_bb_add_inning_next_ppp_tt
	}
	add_to_variable = { KOR_baseball_inning = 1 }
	KOR_bb_inning_icon_clamp = yes
	KOR_bb_reset_outcount_effect = yes
	KOR_bb_clr_all_base_effect = yes
	hidden_effect = {
		country_event = korea.853
	}
}

			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 0 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 1 }

			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 1 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 0 }

		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
		KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
		KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 0 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
		KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 0 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 2 }
			check_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 0 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 2 }
		KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
	}

KOR_bb_go_base_total_effect = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_bb_go_base_total_effect_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { baseball_top_bottom_var = 1 }
		}
		KOR_bb_dpk_go_base_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		KOR_bb_ppp_go_base_effect = yes
	}	
}	
KOR_bb_dpk_out_someone_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				KOR_bb_base_3_var = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				KOR_bb_base_2_var = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				KOR_bb_base_1_var = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_bb_base_1_var = 0 }
	}
	KOR_bb_add_outcount_effect = yes
}

KOR_bb_homerun_effect = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_bb_homerun_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					KOR_bb_base_1_var = 0
				}
			}
		}
		KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					KOR_bb_base_2_var = 0
				}
			}
		}
		KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					KOR_bb_base_3_var = 0
				}
			}
		}
		KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
	}
	KOR_bb_add_score_effect = yes
}

KOR_bb_base_startup_effect = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_bb_base_startup_effect_tt
	clr_country_flag = KOR_bb_cold_game_flag
	clr_country_flag = KOR_bb_end_game_flag
	set_country_flag = KOR_baseball_start_flag
	set_variable = { KOR_base_up_total_score_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_base_down_total_score_var = 0 }
	set_variable = { KOR_baseball_now_game_var = 1 } # Game Setting / 1 Top Start
	set_variable = { KOR_baseball_inning = 1 } # Inning
	set_variable = { baseball_top_bottom_var = 1 } # Inning Icon / Top = 1, Bottom = 2
	KOR_bb_reset_outcount_effect = yes
	KOR_bb_clr_all_base_effect = yes
	KOR_bb_clr_all_score_effect = yes
}
